Corporation ("Truist") reviewed the compensation of Vice Chair and named executive officer, Hugh S. Cummins III, in connection with his recently expanded responsibilities, including leadership of Truist's Corporate Development Group, which includes Truist Ventures, and the Truist Integrated Relationship Management team. The Committee also reviewed Mr. Cummins' target long-term incentive compensation opportunity (including performance share units, cash long-term incentive plan awards and restricted stock units) and supports an increase to 585% of his base salary and will take this revised target into account when making the next round of long-term incentive awards to executive officers in early
